72. ADVANCED — ACTIVITY LOG

72.001 Create /app/:projectId/activity-log. 72.002 Provide an
immutable-style operational history for project activity where
technically appropriate. 72.003 Record user actions. 72.004 Record agent
actions. 72.005 Record workflow actions. 72.006 Record integration
actions. 72.007 Record deployment actions. 72.008 Record database
changes. 72.009 Record secret-management events without secret values.
72.010 Record billing events. 72.011 Record domain changes. 72.012
Record security changes. 72.013 Record marketplace changes. 72.014
Record embed changes. 72.015 Record Git synchronization events. 72.016
Record media-generation events. 72.017 Record file events. 72.018 Record
permission changes. 72.019 Record approval decisions. 72.020 Record
failed operations. 72.021 Record successful operations. 72.022 Record
canceled operations. 72.023 Record system-generated events. 72.024 Show
actor. 72.025 Show actor type. 72.026 Show timestamp. 72.027 Show
resource. 72.028 Show action. 72.029 Show result. 72.030 Show
correlation ID. 72.031 Show request ID where available. 72.032 Show job
ID where available. 72.033 Provide filtering. 72.034 Provide date range.
72.035 Provide actor filter. 72.036 Provide resource filter. 72.037
Provide action filter. 72.038 Provide result filter. 72.039 Provide
export for authorized administrators. 72.040 Redact credentials and
sensitive payloads. 72.041 Prevent ordinary users from viewing
organization-wide logs. 72.042 Prevent log tampering through ordinary
project APIs. 72.043 Retain logs according to configured policy. 72.D001

73. CONTROL CENTER CROSS-PAGE BEHAVIOR

73.001 The selected project must remain visible in the Control Center
header.73.002 Project switching must not silently discard unsaved
configuration.73.003 Unsaved changes must produce a navigation warning
where appropriate.73.004 Breadcrumbs must show the current project and
current Control Center section.73.005 Every page must expose a
consistent Save, Cancel, Reset, or Apply pattern appropriate to its
operation.73.006 Destructive operations must use explicit
confirmation.73.007 Confirmation dialogs must state the target and
consequence.73.008 Long-running operations must move into the durable
job system.73.009 Jobs must remain active when the user leaves the
page.73.010 Returning to a page must restore current job status.73.011
Navigation must support keyboard operation.73.012 Navigation must
support mobile touch interaction.73.013 Collapsible category headings
must preserve expanded state where appropriate.73.014 Active navigation
must clearly indicate the current page.73.015 Navigation must not expose
unauthorized sections as actionable controls.73.016 Disabled features
may be shown as unavailable only when product policy calls for
discoverability.73.017 Tooltips must explain unfamiliar controls without
replacing visible labels.73.018 The sidebar must support search or
command-palette access to deep pages.73.019 Every page must have a
direct URL.73.020 Every direct URL must restore the selected project
context.73.021 Browser refresh must preserve route state.73.022 Back and
forward navigation must behave predictably.73.023 Page transitions must
not leak project data between contexts.73.024 Project context must be
validated server-side for every API call.73.025 Workspace membership
must be checked before project access.73.026 Role permissions must be
checked before configuration changes.73.027 Approval policy must be
checked before consequential operations.73.028 All privileged actions
must create audit events.73.029 Error states must include a recoverable
action when one exists.73.030 The Control Center must remain usable when
an external provider is temporarily unavailable. ## 74. CONTROL CENTER

75. CONTROL CENTER BACKEND RESOURCE RULES

75.0001 Backend rule 1: Every Control Center resource must carry
workspace ownership. 75.0002 Backend rule 2: Every Control Center
resource must carry project ownership when project-scoped. 75.0003
Backend rule 3: Every mutation must validate the caller’s role. 75.0004
Backend rule 4: Every mutation must validate the target resource’s
ownership. 75.0005 Backend rule 5: Every external invocation must
resolve an authorized integration. 75.0006 Backend rule 6: Every secret
reference must resolve server-side. 75.0007 Backend rule 7: Every job
must carry an initiating actor. 75.0008 Backend rule 8: Every job must
carry an idempotency key when the operation can be repeated. 75.0009
Backend rule 9: Every job must emit lifecycle events. 75.0010 Backend
rule 10: Every generated artifact must carry provenance. 75.0011 Backend
rule 11: Every configuration change must be versionable where rollback
is meaningful. 75.0012 Backend rule 12: Every production-affecting
mutation must be distinguishable from development mutations. 75.0013
Backend rule 13: Every public resource must have an explicit visibility
state. 75.0014 Backend rule 14: Every share link must have an explicit
expiration or retention policy where supported. 75.0015 Backend rule 15:
Every provider result must be normalized before entering core project
state. 75.0016 Backend rule 16: Every external failure must be mapped to
a normalized error class. 75.0017 Backend rule 17: Every audit event
must avoid secrets. 75.0018 Backend rule 18: Every API must return
consistent authorization failures. 75.0019 Backend rule 19: Every
asynchronous operation must be resumable or explicitly marked
non-resumable. 75.0020 Backend rule 20: Every UI state must be derivable
from backend state rather than optimistic success alone. 75.0021 Backend
rule 21: Every Control Center resource must carry workspace ownership.
75.0022 Backend rule 22: Every Control Center resource must carry
project ownership when project-scoped. 75.0023 Backend rule 23: Every
mutation must validate the caller’s role. 75.0024 Backend rule 24: Every
